Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The approximation operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 775. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 105 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on approximation structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 775. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 105. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the approximation structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 443. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 55 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the approximation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^443 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 55-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
